4a1(1): @#/ O /ma lus par khong du chud par bya ba'o//da ltar byung ba'i dus la ni mtha' dang dbus med pa ste|nam mkha'i dbyings kyis klas pa//
4a2(1): chos kyi dbyings kyi mtha' gtugs pa ste|de yang ma lus par khong du chud pa ra bya'o//sangs rgyas kyi yul ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i yul te|de kyang thams cad khong du 
4a3(1): chud par bya ba'o//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa ni grangs med pa ste|de dag thams cad kyang khong du chud par bya 'a'o//sangs rgyas kyis thugs su chud pa dang dgongs 
4a4(1): pa'i yul mtha' yas pa yul brjod du med pa ste|de dag kyang thams cad khong du chud par bya ba'o//chos su dmigs pa thams cad ni sems kyi rang bzhin mthar phyin pa (cir )pa ste|
4a5(1): de dag (kyang )thams cad lhag ma med par khong du chud par (1)bya ba'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na;'di lta ste dper na/yid bzhin gyis nor bu las ni/ci bsam pa thams cad 'byung yang 
4a6(1): de snyed du mngon par 'du byed pas/stong par mi 'gyur zhing bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur yang/rgyun chad med do//zad par 'gyur ba med do//yid bzhin
4a7(1): gyi nor bu de yang rgyun gcod par sems pa med do//de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yang sems sgyu ma lta bu la zhugs pas chos kyi dmigs pa thams cad mngon par sgrub kyang mthar 
4a8(1): phyin pas sems mi bskyed de/ting nge 'dzin de dag gis rgyun yongs su mi gcod do//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' ni sgyu ma'i dkyil 'khor yangs shing rgya che
4b1(1): ba tshad med pa la zhugs pas kun tu bzang po'i spyod pa don yod pa nges par rtogs pa la gnas pa dang/mngon par bsgrub pa khong du chud pa'i ye shes kyi sgo dang ldan pas de la rgyas par 'gyur ba 'aM 
4b2(1): 'bri bar 'gyur ba med do//'di lta ste dper na/byis pa so so'-i skye bo rnams ni sems kyi sgo mu med pa ste;de dag gi sems ni ma skyes mi skye skye bar mi 'gyur ba yin/yang de 
4b3(1): dag gi sems skye ba dang skye bar 'gyur ba yang mtha' med pa ste;thug pa med pa'o//zad par mi 'gyur ba'o//sems kyi rgyud yongs su rgyun chad pa med de;bsam gyis mi khyab bo//
4b4(1): de bzhin du byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin gyi rnam pa sgyu ma'i sgo mang po dang ldan pa 'di lta bu la snyoms par zhugs pa la yang mur thug pa med do;tshad kyis gcal ba med do//de ci'i
4b5(1): phyir zhe na;byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i de snyed du tshad med pa'i chos kyi tshul gyi sgo khong du chud pa'i phyir ro//'di lta ste dper na/klu'i rgyal po rgya mtsho chen dang;dga' 
4b6(1): bo dang;bsnyen dga' bo dang;yid du 'ong ba dang/klu'i rgyal po chen po de dag ma yin pa dag gi char phab na char pa'i thig pa re re zhing ba ru ra'i 'bras bu tsam du rgyun du phab kyang char pa de
4b7(1): la zad ces bya ba med do//sngar bab pa de nyid phyir 'bab ces bya ba medo//sprin gyi mngon pa 'du bya ba med do//de bzhin du byang chub semasadapa' kun tu bzang po' ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo dang ;
4b8(1): ye shes kyi sgo dang ;chos kyi sgo dang;sangs rgyas su blta ba'i sgo dang;phyogs su 'gro ba'i sgo dang;sems kyi dbang gis rjes su 'gro ba'i sgo dang ;byin gyis rlabs kyi sgo
20a1(1): @#/ O /mgon po rnams 'byung ste//snod kyi tshul bzhin chos kyi char yang 'bebs//snang ba chen po'i blo gros 'di ston to////
20a2(1): 'phags pa sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo////bam po gnyis pa////de la lha'i rgyal po dge bar grags pa'i blo gros kyi rnam par   
20a3(1): thar pa ni/sems can thams cad rnam par thar pa'i tshogs kyi tshul rnam par ltar 'dzud pa zhes bya'o//lha'i rgyal po mchog tu snang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/lha'i bu rang 
20a4(1): bzhin rnam par rig pa'i 'od ces bya'o//lha'i bu rab tu zhi ba'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni /thabs kyi tshul dang sgo thams cad kyis sangs rgyas kyi (yul)yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya'o//lha'i 
20a5(1): bu dbyangs kyi ri bo'i rnam par thar pa ni /'khor ba rgya mtsho'i phyi ma'i mtha' thams cad du 'gro ba yongs su sgyur ba zhes bya'o//lha'i bu dran pa dge ba'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni /de bzhin
20a6(1): gshegs pa'i tshul rjesu dran zhing /sems can 'dul ba'i byin gyi brlabs zhes bya'o//lha'i bu dga' ba dam pa rnam par snang ba'i rnam par thar pa na /gzungs rgya mtsho la sgo kun nas 
20a7(1): rab tu brtan pa rgya mtsho'i rjes su 'gro ba zhes bya'o//lha'i bu 'jig rten dbyangs kyi blo gros kyi rnam par thar pa ni /sangs rgyas skye ba shin tu dkon pa'i bsam pa bskyed pas mos pa sbyong 
20a8(1): ba'i snying po zhes bya'o//lha'i bu gzi brjid 'bar ba'i bdag po'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad la chos kyi shugs kyis dga' ba bskyed cing bsgrol ba zhes bya'o//
20b1(1): lha'i bu chos kyi dga' bas mas 'phrul bar thar pa ni /byang chub sems dpa'i 'dul ba mtha' yas pa thams cad nam mkha' ltar zad mi shes pa'i sgor rab tu 'jug pa zhes bya'o//lha'i bu
20b2(1): 'phrul bas shin tu 'phags pa'i rnam par thar pa ni /sems can gyi nyon mongs pa mtha' yas pa snying dang /ye shes kyis rgyas par 'gengs pa zhes bya'o//de nas lha'i rgyal po dge bar
20b3(1): grags pa'i blo gros kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus/dge chung dang /tshad med dge dang /dge rgyas kyi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs
20b4(1): su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//chags med spyod pa'i chos nyid 'di la ltos//mi zad phyogs rnams rab tu rgyas bkang ste/bsam gyis mi khyab sangs rgyas yul yang ston//rnam par 
20b5(1): tshul gyi rgya mtsho zad mi shes//bder gshegs 'jig rten kun la mi gnas te//chos dbyings dam pa kun nas 'byung ba med//mig g.yor spyod yul zhing rnams thams cad de/tshul 
20b6(1): 'di mchog tu snang ba'i lha yi'o//bskal pa rgya mtshor thabs la gnas pas na//sangs rgyas 'jig rten kun na rnam par mdzes//chos dbyings de bzhin nyid la rab tu gnas//
20b7(1): de la rab zhi dpal gyi lha 'jug go//sems can gti mug rab rib sgrib pas bsgribs//'khor ba'i spyod yul dngos gyur de dag la//rgyal ba dge ba'i las rnams rab ston te//
20b8(1): rnam thar de la dbyangs kyi ri bo 'jug/de bzhin gshegs pa'i bla na med pa'i tshul/sems can kun gyi bsam par mi nus te/nam pa thams cad rgyal ba rab tu ston /
50a1(1): @#//po che'i rigs thams cad 'byung zhing /byang chub kyi sems rab tu skye ba zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lha ri rab sra ba'i rdo rje sna tshogs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems 
50a2(1): rgya mtsho mi sgul ba'i bsod nams la rnam par lta ba zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lta 'brug sgra sgrog pa rgya mtsho'i shugs kyi dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/ting nge 'dzin gyi dkyil 'khor thams 
50a3(1): cad chos kyi dbyings su yang dag par 'jug pa'i rgyal mtshan zhes bya'o//de nas rgya mtsho'i lha rin po che'i 'od 'byung ba'i dpal gyis/sangs rgyas kyi mthus/rgya mtsho'i lha'i 'khor 
50a4(1): gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//bskal pa rgya mtsho bsam gyis mi khyab par//sangs rgyas rgya mtsho 
50a5(1): rgya chen mang mchod cing //'gro ba dag la bsod nams mtsho spel bas//de phyir rgyal ba lham me lhang nge'o//'gro ba kun tu rgyal ba mngon par mdzes//rdo rje sems dpa' sra ba'i 
50a6(1): dpal dga'o//'gro ba kun tu 'jig rten mgon po ni//chos kyi sprin las char chen yangs pa 'bebs//sdug bsngal rgya mtsho zhi mdzad zad mi shes//rdul dang dri med 'di'i mtshan la 
50a7(1): 'jug/sems can thams cad mnyam med nyon mongs bsgribs//lam rgyud rgya mtsho'i sdug bsngal nang na 'khor//de dag la ni rgyal ba'i yul ston te//dpal bcu'i lha ni 'di la rab 
50a8(1): tu dga'//bskal pa rgya mtsho mang po mtha' yas su//rgyal bas spyod pa mi zad rnams spyad de//'gro ba'i gti mug dkyil 'khor rnam par bzlog/rin chen zla ba'i dpal ni 'di la 
50b1(1): 'jug/'khor ba'i rgya mtsho sdug bsngal chen po ni//sems can 'khor zhing mi bde kun gzigs nas//rgyal bas bla na med pa'i lam yang bstan//klu dpal pad+ma'i gtsug 
50b2(1): bud 'di la dga'//sangs rgyas spyod yul bsam gyis mi khyab ltos//chos kyi dbyings dang nam mkha'i mtshan nyid de//'gro ba'i gti mug dra ba rnam par sbyong //de skad chu 'dzin   
50b3(1): shugs ni smra zhing brjod//sangs rgyas dri med bsam gyis mi khyab spyan//'jig rten kun gyi yul du rnam par mdzes//spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor 'gro ba kun la ston//sna tshogs dpal 
50b4(1): gyi me tog spyod yul lo//bdud kyi dkyil 'khor mi zad rgya che ba'ang //'byam klas thugs kyi skad cig gcig gis btul//rgyal ba bsam kyis mi khyab mi g.yo ste//rdo rje rdul 
50b5(1): 'dzin tshul ni 'di yin no//chos kyi dbyings ni 'byam klas kun bkang zhing //phyogs bcu rnams su rgyal ba'i gsung yang grag/sangs rgyas spyod yul ting nge 'dzin las skyes//
50b6(1): 'di ltar 'di la sprin byung rab tu 'jug/de la chu klung gi lha chu bo kun dang ldan pa'i rnam par thar pa ni//chos kyi chu bo mtha' med pas rgyas par 'gengs pa zhes bya'o//chu klung gi lha ri dang chu 
50b7(1): kun la rten pa'i rnam par thar pa ni//nyon mongs pa thams cad spang ba la yang dag par 'jug pa zhes bya'o//chu klung gilha rdul thams cad dri ma med par rnam par dag pa'i mig gi rnam par
50b8(1): thar pa ni//sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i rdul dang 'grogs pa'i snying rje'i tshul gyis kun tu rgyas par 'gebs pa zhes bya'o//chu klung ga lha nga ros khyab par byed pa'i rnam  
51a1(1): @#//par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi sgra yid du 'ong ba zlog cing phan pa skyed pa zhes bya'o//chu klung gi lha srog chags kyi rgyud mtha' yas pa skyob+sa 
51a2(1): pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems canathams cad la 'tshe ba med pa'i byams pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//chu klung gi lta gdung ba med pa rgya mtsho gdung ba med pa'i 'od dri ma med pa'i rnam par 
51a3(1): thar pa ni/dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad gdung bar mi nus pa'i sgo kun nas ston pa zhes bya'o//chu klung gilha dga' bde kun nas 'byung ba'i rnam par thar pa ni//sems can thams cad
51a4(1): kyi 'jungs pa sel cing gtong ba phun sum tshogs pa yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya'o//chu klung ga lha yon tan gyi shugs yangs pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/thams cad 
51a5(1): tshim par byed pa'i bsod nams kyi zhing rab tu 'byung ba zhes bya'o//chu glang gi lha 'jig rten thams cad kyi dpal rnam par snang ba'i rnam par thar pa na//sems can thams 
51a6(1): cad kyi nyon mongs pa rnam par byang bar byed cing /'khon tu 'dzin pa dam po dang /khro ba'i shugs dang bar byed pa zhes bya'o//chu klung (nga)gi lha tshogs chen po rgya mtsho'i dpal gyi  
51a7(1): rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad bde ba phun sum tshogs pa myong bar byed cing /thar pa rgya mtsho la 'jug pa zhes bya'o//de nas chu klung gi lha chu bo kun dang ldan pas/
51a8(1): sangs rgyas kyi mthus/chu klung gi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod ba thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad (pa)'di dag smras so//chos rnams rgya mtsho mtha' yas spyod pa dag/
51b1(1): sngar chad rgyal bas sems can don tu sbyangs//'jig rten bkang zhing nyon mongs gdung ba rnams//sprin chen char bzhin rab tu zhi bar mdzad//bskal pa brjod du med par rgyal bas ston//
51b2(1): smon lam 'od kyis sems can dbyings sbyangs shing //'gro ba yongs smin dbang po chos kyang mkhyen//'khyil pa zhes bya chu klung lha dga' 'o//'gro dang mnyam pa'i snying rje'i tshul bsgoms
51b3(1): shing //mgon po sems can nyon mongs dri ma sbyong //yongs smin sems can rgyal ba mngon tu lta//de la dri med mig gi lha yang mos//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyangs snyan rgya chen ni//kun tu 
51b4(1): bsgrags pas 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal zhing //sangs rgyas dbyangs snyan thos nas dga' mgu'i yid//rnam thar 'di ni sgra dbyangs sgrogs pas thos//bskal pa rgya mtshor 'gro la phan don tu/
51b5(1): sngan chad byang chub spyod pa spyad pas mthong //de phyir de ni phyogs bkang 'jig rten mdzes//lam rgyud skyob pa rjes su dran zhing dga'//rgyal ba sngan chad spyad pa spyod pa'i tshe//sna tshogs sgo nas
51b6(1): sems can smin par mdzad//de dag bsod nams rgya mtsho sdug bsngal 'jil//zhi byed chu klung lha ni des dga'o//sbyin pa'i sgo mo tshad med mi zad mang //'gro ba 'dod pa kun la yongs btang zhing //
51b7(1): rgyal bas 'gro ba'i 'jungs pa yongs su bsal//kun nas dga' dpal rnam thar de mthong ngo //thabs tshul rgya mtsho thams cad bsgoms pas na//yon tan mang po brgya phrag mang pos byung //
51b8(1): rgyal bas 'gro ba thams cad tshim mdzad de//rgya chen sgra dbyangs shugs ldan lha yang dga'//sgrib pa'i klong dang 'gro ba'i nyon mongs dri//'gro ba rnams la byams pa bskyed sbyangs pas//mkha'
52a1(1): @#//mnyam 'od kyis sangs rgyas rnam par mdzes//rnam pa mdzes pa'i chu klung lha dga'o//sangs rgyas rang byung yon tan rgya mtsho ste//bsod nams zhing ni 'dus 
52a2(1): byas sdug bsngal 'dor//byang chub lam la rim gyis yongs smin mdzad//rgya mtsho'i tshogs dpal rnam thar 'di yin no//de la lo tog gi lha dang 'jam pa'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni//ro dang 
52a3(1): mdangs thams cad skyed cing sems can thams cad sangs rgyas kyi gzugs kyi sku rab tu thob pa ston pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lha dus kyi me tog rnam par mdzes pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni//
52a4(1): 'gro ba thams cad nye bar 'tsho bas dga' chen po'i shugs kyi tshim pa bskyed pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gis lha rtsal dam pa'i stobs kyi rnam par thar pa ni//chos thams cad kyi dbyangs yongs su rgyas pas 
52a5(1): shin tu dag pa'i spyod yul zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lta mdangs kyi ngang mtha' yas pa rab tu 'gyed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/snying rje mtha' yas pa'i sgo nas sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa rab tu 'gyed 
52a6(1): cing rjes su dran pa zhes bya'o//lo tog ga gi lha sa kun gyi rtsa ba 'bras bu'i rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas thams cad mngon du lta zhing sangs rgyas rnams la yon tan yang dag par bskyed pa ston 
52a7(1): pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gi la gtsug pud kha dog sna tshogs kyi rnam par thar pa ni 'gro ba thams cad kyi mos pa'i me tog yongs su sbyong ba zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lha yal ga dri ma med 
52a8(1): par grub pa'i rnam par thar pa ni sems can thams cad la phan pa'i byams pa dang /bsod nams rgya mtsho chen po rnam par 'phel bar byed pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gis lha spos sna tshogs shin 
52b1(1): tu grub pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/chos thams cad la spyod pa kun tu gnon pas rtsom pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lha bzang zhing yid du 'ong bar snang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad kyi  
52b2(1): zhum pa dang//yid du mi 'ong ba rnam par zlog cing /chos kyi dbyings thams cad yongs su dag par byed pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gilha kha dog rnam par dag cing 'od dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni 
52b3(1): 'gro ba thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba la rnam par lta zhing /chos kyis 'khor gyi dbang po tshim par byed pa zhes bya'o/de nas lo tog gi lha dang 'jam pa'i dpal gyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus//
52b4(1): lo tog gi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rang 'byung ye shes sgron ma skyes pa ni/'gro ba kun gyi skyabs dang mgon
52b5(1): gyur te//ma lus 'gro ba'i bde ba yang dag skyed//bsod nams rgya mtsho sangs rgyas bla na med//sangs rgyas mnyan na don yod yon tan sprin//'gro ba kun gyi sdug bsngal zhi bar mdzad//
52b6(1): chos rgya chen po mi zad rab tu skyed//dus kyi dkyil 'khor lha ni 'di la 'jug/bde gshegs stobs ni ma lus yongs su rgyas//'gro ba dag na chos kyi legs pas mdzes//'gro ba kun
52b7(1): 'gro ba kun 'dul kun tu rnam par snang //stobs dang gzugs kyi mthu yis de mthong ngo //sems kyi skad cig kun la 'gro mnyam pa'i//thugs rje rgya mtsho sngan chad rnam sbyangs pas//
52b8(1): de phyir rdzu 'phrul rnam 'phrul mtha' yas so//de ni mdangs mthong lha yis rab tu mthong //thabs rnams kun gyi sgo nas don yod par//sangs rgyas 'jig rten kun tu khyab mdzad cing //
53a1(1): @#//'gro ba kun gyi nyon mongs dag par mdzad//de ni thams cad sgo mo'i rnam thar to//'gro ba 'di na mos yangs gang yod pa//de dag thams cad rgyal ba'i mthus byung ste//de bzhin gsheg+sa
53a2(1): pa'i ye shes rgya mtsho'o//nags tshal rgyan gyi gtsug pud de la 'jug/'jig rten rnams la byams pa che bskyed de//sems can rnams la phan phyir rang byung skyes//lam rab dge 
53a3(1): ba rab tu ston//zhi ba sna tshogs dpal gyi rnam thar to//phyogs su sems can yongs smin byed pa'i tshe//bde gshegs spyod pa ji ltar rnam dag pa//byang chub shing las de dag 
53a4(1): sgra yang 'byung //spos zhim dpal gyi lha yis de thos so//rgyal ba 'jig rten yid du myong ba spong //dga' ba ma lus rnams kyang rab tu skyed//'gro ba kun gyi dbang po rnam par sbyong //
53a5(1): dge bar snang ba'i lha yis de mthong ngo //'gro ba kun na sangs rgyas shin tu 'phags//skye bo kun gyi bsam pa des mkhyen te//sna tshogs sgo nas 'gro ba yongs smin mdzad//rnam 
53a6(1): dag kha dog 'od kyi rnam thar to//de la sman gyi lha phun sum tshogs pa'i rnam par thar pa ni//bsam pa kun nas rnam par lta ba'i gnon pas 'gro ba rab tu gnon pa zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha 
53a7(1): tsan dan gyi nags tshal gyi shugs 'chang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/blta ba don yod pas sems can 'dren pa'i 'od ces bya'o//sman gyi lha 'od rdul med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems 
53a8(1): can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa rab tu zhi bas rdul dang bral ba'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha ming kun tu grags pa glang po'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni//ming bsgrags pas
53b1(1): dge ba'i rtsa ba rgya mtsho yang dag par 'phel ba zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha spu'i glong kun nas 'od 'byung ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/nad thams cad la snying rje'i dkyil 'khor kyi shugs kyi rgyal 
53b2(1): mtshan zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha mun pa rnam par sel cing rnam par sbyong ba'i rnam par thar pa ni /'gro ba mdongs pa lta ba thams cad kyi ye shes kyi mig rnam par dag par byed pa zhes 
53b3(1): bya'o//sman gyi lha sgra dbyangs kun tu 'byung ba'i rnam par thar ba ni/sangs rgyas kyi chos rab tu bsgrags pas don gyi dbyings 'byung ba zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha'i ma'i 'od 'gro ba'i 
53b4(1): mchog gi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad la dge ba'i bshes su ston cing /dge ba'i rtsa bas yang dag par skul ba zhes bya'o//sman gyi lta phyogs bcur rmongs 
53b5(1): pa med par lta ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/mos pa'i tshul gyis rnam par dag pa'i snying rje'i snying po zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha mdangs bzang po kun tu 'gyed pa'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/
53b6(1): sems can thams cad kyi nad rab tu zhi bar byed cing sangs rgyas rjes su dran par sbyor ba zhes bya'o//de nas sman gyi lha phun sum tshogs pas sangs rgyas kyi mthus sman gyi lha'i   
53b7(1): 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas ye shes bsam gyis mi khyab ltos//'gro ba 
53b8(1): kun gyi bsam pa rab tu mkhyen//thabs kyi rnam pa sna tshogs sgo dag nas//sdug bsngal kun las sems can rab tu 'grel//sangs rgyas chos nyid de ltar bsam mi 
54a1(1): @#//khyab|/de yi mthu ni sems can don kyang yod//skye dgu rnams kyi sdug bsngal zhi bar mdzad//tsan dan shugs dang ldan pa'i rnam thar to//sngan   
54a2(1): chad rgyal ba'i chos nyid dag la ltos//bskal pa rgya mtsho mtha' med spyod pa de'ang //srid pa kun la chags pa gang yang med//de la rdul med 'od kyi lha 'jug go/rgyal ba 
54a3(1): bskal pa stong phrag brgya dag tu//mthong zhing mtshan thos de bzhin shin tu dkon//tshul rnams kun tu rang byung don yod de//de la rgyal mtshan mchog ni de ltar 'jug/rgyal   
54a4(1): ba'i spu'i sgo rnams kun nas kyang //nad las rab tu 'grel pa'i 'od bkye bas//'jig rten nyon mongs ma lus zhi bar mdzad//spu mdangs rgyun gyi rnam thar 'di yin no//
54a5(1): sdug bsngal las dang nyon mongs sna tshogs pas//'gro ba rtag tu mdongs shing rab tu rmongs gyur rnams//sangs rgyas ye shes 'od kyis rnam par sbyong //de ni mun dang bral ba'i 
54a6(1): lha mthong ngo //rgyal ba'i gsung dbyangs gcig las mtha' yas pa'i//chos kyi sgo mo thams cad kun kyang 'byung //de ni sems can ma lus kun kyang shes//'di ni sgra dang ldan 
54a7(1): pa'i rnam thar to//rgyal ba'i ye shes bsam gyis mi khyab ltos//srid par sems can yongs su smin byas pa//de la 'gro kun mi mgon mngon tu snang //nyid 'od zhi ba'i lha 
54a8(1): yis de mthong ngo //sangs rgyas thugs rje'i tshul ni rgya mtsho ste//'gro ba kun phan don tu de skyes so//'gro ba kun la lam rnams shin tu ston//de ni phyogs su snang ba'i lhas sh+sa 
54b1(1): so//sangs rgyas 'gro dbang rnams kyi 'od yangs pa//phyogs rnams kyi thams cad ma lus kun tu gang //sangs rgyas rjesu sa dran pa'i gzi brjid skyed//mdangs dang 'od du ldan pa'i rnam thar 
54b2(1): to//de la nags tshal gyi lha yal ga sna tshogs kyi sprin gyi rnam par thar pa ni/ye shes kyi rgya mtsho yangs shing mtha' yas pa'i snying po zhes bya'o//nags tshal gyi lha 'od kyi phung po sna tshogs kyi 
54b3(1): mig yid du 'ong ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/dge ba rgya chen po kun nas spungs pa'i shugs zhes bya'o//nags tshal gyilha myu gu yid du 'ong ba'i 'od gyi rnam par thar pa ni/mos pa sna 
54b4(1): tshogs kyi myu gu rnam par 'phel ba dang /yongs su dag par byed pa zhes bya'o//nags tshal gyis lha lo ma dri ma med pa'i phreng ba'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/yon tan gyi dkyil 'khor thams 
54b5(1): cad kyi rgyan yongs su sbyong zhing rnam par sgom pa zhes bya'o//nags tshalagyi lha yan lag rab tu 'phyang ba'i gzi brjid kyi rnam par thar pa ni /shes rab dri ma med pa'i sgo kun nas chos 
54b6(1): kyi dbyings la dmigs shing /kun nas rnam par dmigs pas rgyas par 'gengs pa zhes bya'o//nags tshal:gyi lha dge ba'i 'od sna tshogs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad 
54b7(1): kyi spyod pa rgya mtsho la yang dag par 'jug pa'i chos kyi sprin mngon par bsgrub pa zhes bya'o//nags tshalal gyi lha sgra skad yid du 'ong ba'i dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can 
54b8(1): thams cad kyi yid du mi 'ong ba'i sgra la bzod pa rab tu thob pas/chos kyi snang ba dri ma med pa zhes bya'o//nags tshan gyi lha kun nas spos mang po ldang ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar 
55a1(1): @#//pa ni;spyod pa rgya chen po'i dkyil 'khor spyod pas yongs dgu par byed cing/phyogs kun tu rnam par snang ba zhes bya'o//nags tshal gyi lha kun nas dpal gyi   
55a2(1): 'od rab tu 'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor spyod  sa yongs dag par byed cing /phyogs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba la phan pa'i don chos thams cad kyi yon tan gyis rgyas pa yang dag par sbyor ba 
55a3(1): zhes bya'o;;nags tshal gyi lha me tog dang /'bras bu dang /ro'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas skye ba thams cad la dga' bar lta zhing rjes su dran pa'i yon tan rgyan gyi snying po zhes 
55a4(1): bya'o;;de nas nags tshal gyi lha yal ga sna tshogs kyi sprin gyis/sangs rgyas kyi mthus/nags tshal gyi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe 
55a5(1): tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//bsod nams stobs dang ye she stobs rnams dang //sngon tshe byang chub spyod pas sangs rgyas byang //stobs rnams kun gyis rang byang pha rol   
55a6(1): phyin;/'gro ba kun na 'phags shing rnam par mdzes//'gro ba thams cad snyed kyi thugs rje'i sgo//de bzhin gshegs pas sngon tshe rab tu sbyangs//rang byung de bas 'gro ba'i   
55a7(1): don mdzad do//sna tshogs spungs 'od rnam thar 'di la 'jug/sems can kun gyis bde gshegs mthong ma thag/mos pa rgya mtsho rnam par dag par 'gyur//rgyal ba kun   gyi   
55a8(1): lam yang yang dag ston//myu gu yid 'ong 'od kyi rnam thar yin//rgyal ba kun gyi ba spu'i yon tan 'khod//bskal pa rgya mtshor zad par mi nus te//'gro ba kun gyis sangs rgyas    
55b1(1): bsam mi khyab//rnam thar 'di ni phreng ba'i dpal gyis mthong //sngan chad rgyal bas sangs rgyas gang gi rnams//zhing mang rdul snyed mchod pa de dag dran//kun tu shes rab dkyil 'khor 
55b2(1): rnam par sbyangs//yal ga 'od kyi brjid kyis 'di mthong ngo //'gro ba kun gyi spyod pa rgya mtsho dag/skad cig gcig gis sangs rgyas thugs su chud//rgyal ba'i ye shes chags med  
55b3(1): shin tu yangs//sna tshogs rgyan gyi 'od kyis 'di mthong ngo /bde gshegs gsung ni zhi ba rab zhir gsungs//dga' ba yangs pa chen po rnam par skyed//sems can mos pa ji   
55b4(1): bzhin de dag shes//'di ni sgra rnams sgrog pa'i dbyangs kyis thos//phyogs bcu'i zhing gi dbyings rnams rgyas byas shing//sangs rgyas byin rlabs rgya chen rab tu 'byung //rgyal ba'i 
55b5(1): sngon gyi spyod pa dag kyang ston//kun tu dri zhim 'od dpal 'di la 'jug/ma rungs sems can yon tan med pa rnams//mos shing 'khor ba kun tu 'khor ba dag/de dag la ni thams cad 
55b6(1): mkhyen lam ston//'phags pa'i 'od kyi 'khor gyis ('di)mthong ngo //sems can ma rungs sbyor bar spyod pa rnams//bskal pa bye bas sangs rgyas mthong bar 'gyur//gzhan ni bde gsheg+sa 
55b7(1): skad cig gcig gis (ba)lta//ro'i 'od kyi lhas ni 'di mthong ngo //de la ri'i lha rin po che'i rtse mo'i me tog shin tu rgyas par gnas pa'i rnam par thar pa ni//bsam gtan zhi zhing yangs 
55b8(1): la dben pa'i snang ba la rab tu 'jug pa zhes bya'o//ri'i lha nags tshal sna tshogs me tog shin tu rgyas pa'i gtsug pud kyi rnam par thar pa ni//'gro ba bsam kyis mi khyab pa'i dge 
56a1(1): @#//ba'i rtsa ba yongs su smin par byed pa'i byams pa sgom pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//ri'i lha kun nas shin tu 'phags pas rnam par snang ba'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par 
56a2(1): thar pa ni;;sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa rnam par lta ba'i dbang po la 'jug pas mngon par snang ba zhes bya'o//ri'i lha rdul thams cad dang mi gnas pas dri ma med pa'i gtsug
56a3(1): pud kyi rnam par thar pa ni//bskal pa rgya mtsho mtha' yas par mi skyo ba'i brtson 'grus kyis rab tu sbyor ba zhes bya'o//ri'i lhi snang bas phyogs su gsal ba'i 'od kyi rnam par 
56a4(1): thar pa ni;sangs rgyas kyi yon tan mtha' yas pa'i snang ba rnam par thar pa ston pa zhes bya'o//ri'i lha stobs la thub pa med pa'i snang ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni sems can yongs 
56a5(1): su smin par bya ba'i phyir/gti mug yongs su sel pa'i 'od ces bya'o//ri'i lha kun nas gzi brjid dpal gyi dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni/chags pa thams cad dang mnyam 
56a6(1): pa'i rgyan gyis sdug bsngal yang dag par 'jom pa zhes bya'o//ri'i lha snang ba'i rdul chu bo'i dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bstan pa'i yon 
56a7(1): tan snang ba yang dag par ston pa zhes bya'o//ri'i lha kun tu mngon par lta ba'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/1 sems can thams cad kyi rmi lam gyi mthar yang dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam par 'phel 
56a8(1): bar byed pa zhes bya'o//ri'i lha kun nas sra ba'i rdo rje'i mtshams kyi mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/don rgya mtsho rgya chen po mtha' yas pa dang 'grogs pa zhes bya'o//de nas ri'i lha rin 
56b1(1): po tshe rtse mo'i me tog shin tu rgyas par gnas pas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus/ri'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras 
56b2(1): so;;rgyal ba'i rnam 'phrul mi zad (chad) med ltos//sngon tshe spyod rab mtha' yas rgyal bas spyad//rnam thar zhing gi rdul snyed bsgom mdzad pas//'gro ba ma lus dga' ba'i shugs kyang 
56b3(1): skyed;;sangs rgyas spu nas dri med dpal bkye ba//yan lag mtshan brgyan 'gro ba kun bkang zhing //byams tshul mtha' yas 'jig rten kun na ston//nags tshal rgyas pa'i 
56b4(1): gtsug gi rnam thar to//sangs rgyas sku ni mtha' yas 'gro bar mngon//'jig rten kun tu phyogs bcu bkang zhing 'phags//dbang po dang ba'i sems can rangs shing dga'//
56b5(1): rnam thar 'di la 'phags pa'i rgyal mtshan 'jug/rang byung mkha' mnyam 'jig rten chos ma chags//bskal pa kun spyod btson 'grus stobs mi skyo//sna tshogs khams 'gro 
56b6(1): sbyor bas ma lus 'dul//dga' ba dri med gtsug pud rnam thar to//'jig rten kun ni mdongs rgyur mun stug zhugs//thugs rje der bskyed sangs rgyas 'od bkye zhing //
56b7(1): 'gro khams yun rings nyal ba sad par mdzad//rig gnas dga' 'od lha'i rnam thar to//srid pa'i rgya mtshor spyad pa sngon spyod tshe//zhing rdul mnyam pa'i sems can gtso mtshong 
56b8(1): pas;;de dag dgyes bzhin sems can byang chub bkod//zhi ba'i sa 'di stobs 'chang lha 'jug pa'o//las kyi sprin bsgrib g.yogs shing bsgribs pa rnams//srid par   
57a1(1): ka @#//sems can sdug bsngal 'khor gzigs nas//de dag mun pa ye shes 'od kyis bsal//gzi brjid dpal 'od rnam thar 'di sems so//
57a2(1): rgyal ba'i spu las sangs rgyas yon tan kun//phyogs dbyings sems can bsam pa zhig tu grag/rdzu 'phrul rnam pa kun tu gnas//'od mang rdul gyi chu bo'i lha 'jug go/
57a3(1): sems can kun spyod dge ba rgya mtsho kun//de la phyogs bkang sangs rgyas mngon par snang //sna tshogs thabs kyis 'jig rten chos kyang ston//tshul mgon mig gi lha yis 
57a4(1): de mthong ngo //chos sgo rgya mtsho mi zad tshad med pa//sangs rgyas gsung gcig sems can kun la gsung //bskal pa kun rdzob 'jig rten mang la ston//rdo rje'i tshul mig 'di'i gnas la   
57a5(1): 'jug//de la sa'i lha dpal gyi me tog kun nas shin tu rgyas pa dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/skad cig skad cig cing sems can thams cad la byams pa dang /snying rje'i dkyil 'khor    
57a6(1): gyis rnam par blta ba zhes bya'o//sa'i lha bsod nams brtan pa shin tu bsags pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi stobs dang /shugs kyi rgyan 
57a7(1): ces bya'o//sa'i lha sems rin po ches rgyas pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/ting nge 'dzin sna tshogs bsgom pas rnam par dpyod pa dri ma med pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha dus dus su dge ba'i mig rab
57a8(1): tu ston pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad dga' zhing bde ba dang /rtse ba'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//sa'i lha gser gyi mig gi dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/lus thams cad yong+sa 
57b1(1): su dag par byed pa'i tshul ston pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha spu las spos kyi 'od rnam par 'gyed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yon tan rgya mtsho thams cad rnam par bsgom 
57b2(1): pa brjod pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha sgra dbyangs yid du 'ong ba sgrog pa'i rnam par thar pa ni /'gro ba thams cad smra ba'i sgra rgya mtsho byin gyis rlob pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha me tog gi glong sna tshogs
57b3(1): kyi gtsug pud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sang rgyas kyi zhing phun sum tshogs pa'i rang bzhin dri ma med pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha kun nas rdo rje'i rdul gyi rgyun gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sang
57b4(1): kyi chos thams cad kyi 'khor lo'i byin gyis rlabs kun nas rgyu zhing 'byung ba zhes bya'o//sa'i lha khyim dri ma med pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni /sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi tshul 
57b5(1): la yang dag par 'jug pa'i chos kyi rgyan bsgom pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//de nas sa'i lha dpal gyi me tog kun nas shin tu rgyas pa dri ma med pas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus sa'i lha'i  
57b6(1): 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sngan chad skad cig skad cig bde gshegs kyis//brjod du med 
57b7(1): pa'i snying rje sgo rnams dang //byams pa'i sgo yang mi zad bsgoms pas na//de phyir sangs rgyas sgo ni mi shigs sra//dus gsum gtogs pa'i sems can bsod nams dang //
57b8(1): ji snyed byang chub sems dpa'i bsod nams dag/rgyal ba'i spu gcig de snyed bsod nams 'od//bsod nams de mthong rab tu dga' bar 'gyur//'byung ba med cing byon 
58a1(1): @#//pa gang yang med//ting 'dzin zhi ba rgya mtshor rab tu zhugs//sems can rgyan gyi sa 'di rab tu ston//'di ni sna tshogs shing gi ze ba'i sa yin no//
58a2(1): sngan chad thub pas spyod pa spyad pa dang //'gro ba'i sgrib pa rnam par zlog pa ni//rin chen bkram pa'i 'od kyi sa yin te//rnam thar de la dga' bar lha lta'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
58a3(1): sa ni mtha' yas te//'jig rten kun tu skad cig skad cig ston//dus tshod yang dag ston pa'i sa yi lha//dga' bar sangs rgyas yul la rnam par lta//sangs rgyas gsung 
58a4(1): ni mtha' yas bsam mi khyab//sems can kun bsgrags nyon mongs zhi bar mdzod//gser 'od mig gi dpal ni rab dga' bar//sangs rgyas yon tan mtha' yas mang 
58a5(1): la lta//sangs rgyas sprul pas rnam pa kun gzugs su//phyogs kun chos kyi dbyings su rgyas par mdzad//spu las dri zhim 'od kyi sa yi lha//sangs rgyas sems can 'dul ba 
58a6(1): de la lta//phyogs rnams yid 'ong dbyangs kyis gang byas shing //'gro ba 'di ni phyi mtha'i bskal par brjod//yid du 'ong ba'i sgra dbyangs sa yi lha//rab tu dga' bar sangs rgyas dbyangs 
58a7(1): la nyan//rgyal ba'i spu las sems can mnyam pa yi//spos kyi 'od bkye 'jig rten kun bkang zhing //sems can ma lus yongs su smin mdzad pa//me tog gtsug pung 'phyang bas yang 
58a8(1): dag mthong //de bzhin gshegs sku rdo rje lta bu ste//mtshams su mi phyed sra ba ri bo 'dra//'gro ba kun gyis sangs rgyas brjod mi nus//rdo rje rdul gyi lha ni des dga'o//de la grong 
58b1(1): khyer gyi lha rin chen rtse mo'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni//sems can la phan pa yang dag par 'byung ba zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lha rgyan rnam pa sna tshogs 'byung ba'i rnam par thar pa ni//
58b2(1): 'gro ba'i dbang po yongs su smin par byed pa'i 'dul ba 'byung ba zhes bya'o//grongakhyer gyi lha rin chen dri med shin tu dga' ba'i rnam par thar pa ni//sems can thams cad bsod 
58b3(1): nams dang /dga' ba myong bar byed pa zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lta slos pa'i mya ngan thams cad spong bas rnam par snang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni /skyob pa dang mi 'jigs pa dang /
58b4(1): snying rje'i snying po zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lga sgron ma shin tu rgyas pa'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/shes rab kyis kun tu rnam par 'byed pa zhes bya'o//grong khyed gyi lha 'od 'phro 
58b5(1): ba'i dpal mngon par 'bar ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/thabs kyi sgo kun nas ston pa'i mthu zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyis lha bsod nams kyi shugs snang ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/
58b6(1): 'gro ba kun gyi bsod nams 'byung bas snang ba zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyilha lus dri ma med par mnyam pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni/sems can rmongs pa thams cad la rmi 
58b7(1): lam rnam par ston pa'i 'od ces bya'o//grong khyer gyi lha spos kyi rgyal mtshan gtsug pud kyi rnam par thar pa ni nyon mongs pa dang /mi dge ba'i dri rnam par sel cing thams cad
58b8(1): mkhyen pa nyid kyi dri yang dag par skyed pa zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lha rin chen dpal gyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa na/'gro ba thams cad kyi sgrib pa'i ri rnam par 'joms pa'i 
59a1(1): @#//'od ces bya'o//de nas grong khyer gyi lha rin po che'i rtse mo'i 'od kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus/grong khyer gyi lha'i dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/
59a2(1): de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//mgon po'i chos nyid bsam gyis mi khyab ste//snang ba brnyes shing phyogs su rnam par mdzes//sems can mtha' yas yongs su smin mdzad
59a3(1): pa'i|/sangs rgyas 'gro ba kun la mngon sum snang //sems can thams cad dbang po sna tshogs te//de dag ma lus rgyal bas 'gro bar mkhyen//rnam thar 'di ni rab tu thob pa'i phyir//
59a4(1): sna tshogs 'od 'byung lha yang rtag tu dga'//rgyal bas bskal pa mtha' yas yang dag spyad//sngan chad rgyal bas chos kyang yang dag bzung //dga' ba'i yid kyis rtag tu de dag blangs//
59a5(1): rnam thar 'di ni dga' ba'i 'od kyis thob//'gro ba 'dir ni thugs rje byams bskyed cing //de bzhin gshegs pas sngan chad sems can rnams//kun gyi mya ngan mtha' yas rnam par   
59a6(1): bzlog/nyon mongs yongs su sbyong ba 'di la dga'//rgyal ba'i shes rab rgya che mtha' yas te//tshad med nam mkha' 'dra zhing mtshungs pa yin//de bzhin gshegs pa'i shes rab rnam   
59a7(1): spyod pas//mar me me tog 'od kyi lha dga'o//de bzhin gshegs pas 'gro mnyam sku gzugs rnams//'gro ba kun gyi bsam pa rnams la bstan//de 'dra'i tshul la rab tu bsgom 
59a8(1): pa yis|/mngon tu 'od 'phro'i lha de rab tu dga'//bsod nams rgya mtsho rgya chen mtha' yas pa//sngan chad de bzhin gshegs pas yongs su sbyangs//de 'dra'i rnam par thar la rnam   
59b1(1): lta bas;/bsod nams dpal gyi 'od de rab tu dga'//srid par rab tu rmongs pa'i sems can rnams//'khor gyur rab ri ba mdongs pa gzigs pas na//de dag phan phyir sangs rgyas   
59b2(1): mngon tu byung //dri med 'od kyis rnam thar 'di thob bo//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdzu 'phrul stobs la ltos//'gro la sprin 'dra mtha' dang dbung yang med//de dag bstan cing 'gro ba rnam   
59b3(1): par 'dul;;spos kyi rgyal mtshan 'od kyis 'di mthong ngo //sems can rmongs pa mun pas mdongs gyur cing //rnam pa sna tshogs sgrib pas bsgribs gyur pa//sangs rgyas 
59b4(1): 'od kyis rnam par kun shes 'gyur//rtse mo'i 'od kyi lha ni 'di la 'jug/de la snying po byang chub kyi lha dge ba dri ma med pa'i rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i rnam par thar pa ni//sangs rgyas mang 
59b5(1): po mchod pa yang dag par 'byung zhing //sngon gyi smon lam gyi rgyan ston pa zhes bya'o//snying po byang chub kyi lha ri rab spo'i rin po che'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad dang mngon 
59b6(1): sum du;byang chub kyi spyod pa yang dag sgrub cing 'byung ba zhes bya'o//snying po byang chub kyi lha dbyangs kyi 'brug sgra sgrog pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi   
59b7(1): bsam pa la sangs rgyas ston cing rmi lam gyi mthar sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi 'brug sgra sgrog pa zhes bya'o//snying po byang chub kyi lha me tog gi char sna tshogs 'bab pa'i mig gi rnam par 
59b8(1): thar pa ni;dka' ba thams cad yongs su gtong zhing rin po che'i char 'bebs pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//snying po byang chubkyi lha 'od 'phro mang po shin tu dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/
60a1(1): @#//'dul ba'i sems can yongs su smin par byed cing //snying po byang chub phun sum tshogs pa'i rnam pa sna tshogs ston pa zhes bya'o//snying po byang chub kyi lha me tog 
60a2(1): gi phreng ba 'od kyi gtsug pud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi dbang po bzhin du chos kyi tshul zab mo dran pa ston pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//snying po byang cub kyi lha'i rin po che'i   
60a3(1): rgyan gyi char rab tu 'bebs pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/dga' ba yang dag par myong ba mtha' yas pa la yang dag par 'jug pa zhes bya'o//snying po bya chub kyi lha mthu dam pa spos kyi   
60a4(1): mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan dang /legs par rab tu brjod pa'i byin gyis rlabs zhes bya'o//snying po byang che ba kyi lha rdo rje sna tshogs kyi sprin gyi rnam par thar 
60a5(1): pa ni;snying po byang chub rnam pa mtha' yas pa yang dag par ston pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//snying po byang chubakyi lha pad+mo'i shing snang ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/byang chub kyi shing yongs su
60a6(1): zhi bas phyogs rgyas par byed pa zhes bya'o//snying po byang chu ba kyi lha dge ba'i lta ba sna tshogs rnam par ston pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs sna tshogs yang
60a7(1): dag par rgyu ba zhes bya'o//de nas snying po byang chub kyi lha dge ba dri ma med pa'i rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi mthus snying po byang chub kyi lha'i 'khor gyi   
60a8(1): dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//bskal pa mang po mtha' dang dbus med par//de bzhin gshegs pas ji ltar 
60b1(1): sngon spyad dran;/sangs rgyas thams cad mchod cing bsgrubs pas na//de phyir de'i bsod nams nam mkha' yi tshad//zhing rnams mtha' yas tshad med 'dra med par//mi zad gtong ba'i 
60b2(1): sgo nas sngon gtang bas//ri bo'i spo'i 'od kyi lha de ni//bde gshegs dran zhing rab tu dga' bar 'gyur//bde gshegs sprul pa rnam pa mtha' yas pa//de dag zhing rnams kun tu rab tu rgyu;;
60b3(1): 'gro bar sangs rgyas rmi lam bzhin ston cing //sprin gyi sgra dbyangs lha de rab tu dga'//mtha' yas bskal par dga' ba rab spyad de//dang ba'i spyan gyis 'gro la phan pa'i phyir//rgya 
60b4(1): mtsho'i spyan yang yongs su sngon btang ste//de la me tog mig gi lha dga'o//yon tan rin chen 'od 'phro mtha' yas sprin//'gro ba kun tu snying po byang chub nas//rab tu bkye ba'i 
60b5(1): sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul gyis//'od 'phro 'gyed pa'i lus kyi lha yang dga'//sems can rnams kyi spyod pa rgya mtsho ste//de la rgyal ba chos kyi char yang 'bebs//ji ltar mos 
60b6(1): par nem nur rnam par sel//de yis gtsug pud 'phyang ba dga' ba thob//rgyan gyi char 'bebs mig bzangs lha de ni//sems kyi skad cig gcig gis tshad med pa'o//chos 
60b7(1): don dbyangs kyi bye brag mtha' yas pa//rgya mtsho so so yang dag rig pas 'jug/zhing rnams brjod du med pa thams cad du//'gro ba kun la sangs rgyas legs brjod pas//de phyir yon   
60b8(1): tan sangs rgyas zhing kun grags//de ni rjes su spos mig lha yang dran//mtha' yas shing gi rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs//byang chub shing la kun kyang ma lus bstan//rdo rje sna tshogs 
61a1(1): @#//sprin gyi lha de ni//byang chub (shing )la dga' bar rnam par blta//sangs rgyas ye shes snying po byang chub la//phyogs rnams kun tu mtha' dang mu med de//pad+ma snang 
61a2(1): ba'i gsal ba'i lha de ni//rnam thar rnam yangs 'di la rab dga'o//sangs rgyas stobs rnams yongs dag bsam mi khyab//snying po byang chub 'di ni kun kyang grag/yang dag 
61a3(1): bsgrubs pas 'byung ba de dag ni//thams cad sna tshogs snang ba'i 'od nyan to//de la rkang gis 'gro rin chen dpal gyi phyag rgya'i rnam par thar pa ni//dga' ba yangs pa rin po che'i 
61a4(1): char gyis kun tu khyab pa zhes bya'o//rkang gis 'gro pad+ma snang ba'i 'od zer gyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi sku 'od zer gyi kha dog pad+ma'i dra ba thams cad du ston pas dga' 
61a5(1): bar byed pa zhes bya'o//rkang gis 'gro me tog gtsug pud gyi dpal dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems kyi skad cig thams cad la de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor 
61a6(1): gyi dbyibs dang rgyan 'byung ba zhes bya'o//rkang gis 'groshin tu mdzes par gnon pa'i rnam par thar pa ni//gom ba 'dor ba thams cad du sems can mtha' yas pa 'dul ba'i   
61a7(1): rgyan ces bya'o//rkang gis 'gro rin chen dpa' gyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/skad cig gcig gis pad+ma'i dra ba sna tshogs sprul pa byed cing /rin po che'i char pa dang /dbyangs 
61a8(1): dang snang ba kun tu 'gyed pa zhes bya'o//rkang gi 'gro dga' ba'i dbyangs snyan pa'i rnam par thar pa ni /dga' ba'i shugs dang nyams bde ba rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa 'byung ba zhes 
61b1(1): bya'o;;rkang gis 'gro shing tsan dan gyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni//'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad du spos kyi ngad sprin du ldang ba zhes bya'o//rkang gis 'gro pad+ma yid du 'ong ba'i 
61b2(1): 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni /pad+ma'i gseb thams cad nas/yid du 'ong ba'i chos kyi dbyangs 'byung bas snang ba bskyed pa zhes bya'o//rkang gis 'gro'i rdul gyi shugs kyi 'od kyi rnam 
61b3(1): par thar pa ni/lus kyi 'od zer rnam pa sna tshogs thams cad kyi dra bas snang bar byed pa zhes bya'o//rkang gi 'gro me tog mang po'i dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can 
61b4(1): thams cad la dge ba'i rtsa ba rgya mtshos yang dag par skul ba 'byung ba zhes bya'o//de nas rkang gis 'gro rin chen dpal gyi phyag rgyas sangs rgyas kyi mthus rkang gis 'gro'i lha'i 'khor 
61b5(1): gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//dga' ba rgya mtsho'i tshul gyi shugs bskyed cing //bskal pa mtha'   
61b6(1): yas sngan chad spyod pa mdzad//zhing rnams kun rdul shin tu 'phra mnyam pa'i//sangs rgyas rnams kyang dgyes pa'i thugs kyis mchod//skad cig sems la pad+ma brjod yangs pa//
61b7(1): dri zhim sna tshogs mngon par sprul mdzad nas//zhing rnams kun tu rgyal ba gshegs mdzad pa//snang ba'i 'od zer lha des rab tu mthong //'dren pa rnams kyi chos nyid bsam mi   
61b8(1): khyab;;'khor rnams kun tu 'byam klas mtha' yas pa'i//rnam par 'phrul ba mi zad rab tu ston//me tog gtsug pud lha des de mthong ngo //phyogs bcu'i zhing gi dbyings   
62a1(1): @#//rnams ma lus pa//thams cad kun tu gshegs shing bzhud pa'i sa//sems can mtha' yas yongs su smin par mdzad//shin tu mdzes pas 
62a2(1): dga' bar de (ma)thong ngo //sems can snyed kyi sprul pa kun 'phros pa//re re'i sku yis chos kyi dbyings kyang 'gengs//rin chen dri med 'od kyang rab tu ston//rkang 'gro dpal gyi 
62a3(1): mtshan gyis 'di mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul mtha' yas te//'khor tu rgyal ba chos kyi char yang 'bebs//bde gshegs de'i 'khor na dgar gnas shing ༐
62a4(1): dga' sgra dbyangs kyis rnam thar de mthong ngo //sangs rgyas dbyangs ni mkha' dang mnyam pa ste//dbyangs kyi yan lag yon tan kun dang ldan//'jig rten kun bkang sems 
62a5(1): can rnam par 'dul//tsan dan shing gi 'od ni de la nyan/ O /spu rtse kun nas rgyal ba rnam 'phrul dang //dus gsum gshegs pa rgyal ba'i mtshan gyi rnams//sgra bsgrags thos shing 
62a6(1): dga' ba skyer 'gyur te//de ni rdo rje 'od kyi lhas mthong ngo //de bzhin gshags sku bsam gyis mi khyab ste//gom pa gom par yon tan rgya mtsho ston//de dag ji ltar sems   
62a7(1): can mos pas mthong //rdul gyi 'od kyi lhas ni de mthong ngo //'jig rten 'gon po rnam 'phrul rnam mang gis//phyogs bcur sems can yang dag skul ba mdzad//me tog 
62a8(1): 'khor mang rkang pas 'gro ba'i lha//mthong zhing dga' ba yangs pa rab tu thob//de la lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha khrel dang dge bas rgyas pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/
62b1(1): de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sngon gyi smon lam rjes su dran pas rnam par dpyod pa zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha 'od zer phyogs su snang pa'i rnam par thar pa ni;
62b2(1): 'jig rten gyi khams mu med par sangs rgyas kyi 'od rnam par ston pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha sgras 'dul ba rgya mtsho'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/
62b3(1): sangs rgyas kyi gsung 'dul ba'i sems can thams cad la brjod cing //yang dag par skul bas dga' ba 'byung ba zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha rgyan bzang po 
62b4(1): me tog gtsug gi rnam par thar pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku nam mkha'i mtshan nyid kyi byin gyi rlabs kun nas rab tu gnas pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris su gtogs 
62b5(1): pa'i lha spyod yul mtha' yas dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yul 'gro ba rnams la yang dag par ston pa'i mthu zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris sa gtogs
62b6(1): pa'i lha snang ba'i phreng ba 'dzin pa'i dpal gyi rnam par thar ba ni/sems can thams cad gso ba dang /'dres pa'i mdangs kyis dga' ba zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris su gtog+sa 
62b7(1): pa'i lha spos dri ma med pa'i sprin las char 'bebs pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i dri ma mngon par sel ba'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//
62b8(1): lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha srung 'dzin yongs su 'dzin pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sem can thams cad kyi bdud kyi las dang /rmongs pa rnam par bzlog pa zhes bya'o//
63a1(1): @#//lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha sgo kun nas yongs su gnon pa la mngon du gyur pa'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/'jig rten gyi dbang po thams cad kyi 
63a2(1): gnas su rgyal ba rnam par dmigs par mngon tu ston pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//lus kyi ris su gtog+sa pa'i lha khrel dang dpal mi g.yo ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems
63a3(1): can thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba'i shugs thams cad yang dag par 'dzin cing yang dag par sbyong ba zhes bya'o//de nas lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'ilha khrel dang dge bas rgyas 
63a4(1): pa'i 'od kyis sangs rgyas pa'i 'od kyi mthus/lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'ilha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag 
63a5(1): smras so;/rib rab rdal 'phran shin tu phra snyed kyi//bskal pa de dag phra snga'i bskal pa ni//rdul ces bya bar 'od mdzes 'od zer rgyal//mchod de smon lam btab (pa) bdag gis dran//
63a6(1): sangs rgyas sku las 'od 'phro rab bkye ba//de'i 'od zer chos kyi dbyings su rgyu//rgyal ba'i 'od zer dro ba sems can 'dul//'od 'phro 'od zer lha yis de mthong ngo//dbyangs kyi 
63a7(1): yan lag yon tan kun rgyas pa'i//sangs rgyas gsung ni zhing rnams kun tu sgrogo/'gro ba ma lus thams cad yang dag skul//dbyangs kyis 'dul ba de la dga' bar nyan//
63a8(1): rgyal ba'i sku ni rab tu zhi la ltos//sku gzugs du ma'ang mtshan nyid mi mnga' ste//'gro ba kun na yang bzhugs par rab tu snang //me tog gtsug gtsug gis ji bzhin nyid du mthong ;
63b1(1): 'dren pa'i chos nyid bsam gyis mi khyab ste//sems can de dag dad pa ji bzhin du//la la bzhugs dang bzhengs dang gshegs par mthong //de yang spyod lam 'byung ba'i 
63b2(1): lhas mthong ngo //rgyal ba'i tshems kyi 'chams rnams thams cad nas//dri zhim 'od 'phro sgron ma'i sprin bkye bas//'gro ba kun gyi nyon mongs zhi bar 'gyur//dri med spos
63b3(1): kyi sprin gyis de mthong ngo //sangs rgyas mgon po 'gro ba'i skyabs su byung //nyon mongs rmongs shing sdug bsngal gzir ba rnams//mgu bar mdzad cing nyon mongs tham+sa 
63b4(1): cad bcil//snang ba'i phreng ba 'chang bas 'di mthong ngo //'dren pa bskal pa brgyar yang shin tu dkon//rang byung 'jig rten don mdzad skyes pa ni//'jig rten kun gyi dbul 
63b5(1): ba rnam par sel//khrel yod dpal gyi 'od snang de skad smra//sems can nyon mongs sgrib pas nyon mongs shing //bdud kyi lam gyis dkrugs te 'phyan pa'i rnams//de dag
63b6(1): rgyal ba'i rnam thar lam ston te//srung zhing skyong ba'i dpal gyis de mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs kyi rnam par 'phrul la ltos//sangs rgyas sprin gyis chos kyi dbyings mnan cing //'jig 
63b7(1): rten dbang po gnas su sems can 'dul//kun sgo'i mig gi dpal gyi spyod yul lo//de la lag na rdo rje mthu chen kha dog bzang po'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni//yon tan mtha' yas 
63b8(1): pa dang gzugs kyi sku rnam par dpyod pa yang dag par ston pa zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rje nyi ma'i shugs kyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi ba spu las nyi   
64a1(1): ka@#//ma'i dkyil 'khor gyi sprin sna tshogs kyi rgyun 'byung ba zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rje ri rab me tog rnam par thar pa ni//rnam par 'phrul ba rgya chen po bstan  
64a2(1): pas yon tan mtha' yas pa'i lus su 'phrul pa zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rja rnam dag sprin gyi dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyangs mtha' yas tshad med   
64a3(1): pa'i rjes su song ba zhes bya'o//lag na rda rje dbang po shin tu mang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/'jig rten gyi dbang po thams cad kyi rnam par 'phrul ba ston cing /sangs rgyas kyi 
64a4(1): byin gyi rlabs kyis yang dag par skul ba zhes bya'o//lag na rdorje rdo rje'i 'od yid du 'ong ba'i rnam par thar pa ni /sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi dbyings rab tu phye ba thams cad ma lus 
64a5(1): pa la yang dag par spyod pa zhes bya'o//lag na rdo+e shing gi sgra dbyangs kyi rnam par thar ba ni/shing gi lha thams cad 'dren pa'i rgyan yid du 'ong bas brgyan pa zhes bya'o//
64a6(1): lag na rdo rje seng ge'i 'od rgyal po'i rnam par thar pa ni/de  bzhin gshegs pa'i bsod nams kyi dkyil 'khor yangs pa'i tshogs kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i 'od ces bya'o//
64a7(1): lag na rdo rje 'od 'phro stug po'i dpal gyi mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can ma rungs pa'i rgyud la lus 'jigs 'jigs ltar rab tu ston cing /kun tu rnam par lta ba zhes 
64a8(1): bya'o|/lag na rdo rje rin chen gtsug phud pad+ma'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/byang chub sems dpa'i rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa'i gtsug gi nor bu rin po che thams cad kyi   
64b1(1): char mngon par 'bebs pa zhes bya'o//de nas lag na rdo rje mthu chen kha dog bzang po'i 'od kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus lag na rdo rje'i 'khor kyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams
64b2(1): cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//chos rgyal chos kyis mdzes pa yi//chos nyid de la rnam par ltos//yon tan mtha' yas sku   
64b3(1): gzugs kyis//'jig rten kun tu rnam par mdzes//sangs rgyas ba spu thams cad nas//'od zer dra ba bsam mi khyab//nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor 'dra ba ste//phyogs 
64b4(1): bcu kun tu rnam par mdzes//sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul de dag gis//chos kyi dbyings su khyab byas shing //'gro ba kun la mngon sum du//mi zad sku ni shin tu ston//
64b5(1): thams cad gsung gi dkyil 'khor ni//zhing rnams kun tu rab tu sgrog/gsung gis sems can thams cad kyi//bsam pa thams cad rab tu 'gengs//'jig rten 
64b6(1): dbang po kun gnas na//thub pa rab tu bzhugs par snang //de dag thams cad 'khor rnams la//'dren pa dag ni chos ston mdzad//rgya mtsho chos kyi sgo bskul bas//
64b7(1): gzhan dang gzhan gyi tshul dag gis//thams cad chos kyi bye brag gnon//rgyal ba zad med rab tu ston//rgya chen mtha' med tshul dag la//rgya mtsho 'khor rnams rab tu   
64b8(1): 'jug;/sangs rgyas 'od zer rab 'dres pas//kun gyis 'dren pa mthong bar 'gyur//zhing rnam bye ba'i rdul snyed du//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kun mchod pas//
65a1(1): @#//bsod nams rgya chen nam mkha' bzhin//kun gyis 'dren pa rab tu mthong //sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul ma 'dres pa//thams cad zhing du ston pa mdzad//
65a2(1): 'gro la sangs rgyas mngon sum du//byang chub snying por rnam par mdzes//'od zer sna tshogs dkyil 'khor gyi//'od 'phro sprin ni kun tu snang //chos dbyings kun tu khyab
65a3(1): byas shing//sangs rgyas spyod yul rab tu ston//bam po lnga pa////de la byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po ni/rnam par 
65a4(1): thar pa'i tshul rgya mtsho la 'jug ste/'di ltar sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad yongs su dag par bya ba dang /'gro ba 'dul ba la mthar thug par mngon par sgrub pa zhes bya ba'i
65a5(1): rnam par thar pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi zhabs kyi drung du phyin pa sa yon tan gyi tshogs thams cad kyi tshogs thams cad kyi yul zhes bya ba'i rnam par thar
65a6(1): pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis nas sar smon lam rgya mtshos rab tu gnas pa 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i rnam par thar pa dang /sgo thams cad nas chos kyi
65a7(1): dbyings su bdag gi lus kyi sprin rdul snyed mtha' yas pas rgyas par 'gengs pa zhes bya ba'i rnam par tharapa dang /zhing thams cad du rang gi ming bsam gyis mi khyab 
65a8(1): pa'i 'khor lo sgrog pa'i byin gyis rlabs zhes bya ba'i rnam par tharpa dang /rdul shin tu phra ba thams cad kyi sbubs su byang chub sems dpa'i yul dang /rnam par 'phrul
65b1(1): pa mtha' dang dbus med pa yang dag par ston pa zhes bya ba'i rnam par thar pa dang /dus gsum gyi bskal pa skad cig gcig tu ston pas 'byung ba dang /'jig pa rab tu 
65b2(1): 'jig pa rab tu 'byung ba zhes bya ba'i rnam par tharapa dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi dbang po rgya mtsho'i yul phan tshun du 'jug pa yang dag par ston pha zhes bya 
65b3(1): ba'i rnam par thar pa dang/gang 'di chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med par rang gi lus kyi sprul pas rgyas pa rnam par 'phrul ba'i byin gyis rlabs zhes bya ba'i rnam par tharas pa dang 
65b4(1): gang 'di byang chub sems dpa'i chos thams cad kyi tshul phan tshun du ston pa rgya chen pos thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi tshul la 'jug pa zhes bya ba'i rnam par thor pa dag go/de nas 
65b5(1): byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos rnam par thar pa'i tshul rgya mtsho bsam gyis mi khyab pa dag gis/de bzhin gshegs pa'i yon tan rgya mtsho la 'jug cing /sangs rgyas 
65b6(1): kyi mthus|'khor rgya mtsho yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas de'i tshe/tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//zhing rnams rgyal bas dri med rnam par sbyangs//
65b7(1): mkha' yangs rdul bzhin yon tan tshad kyang med//sngon gyi rgyal ba'i sras po dri med pa//de la bsam yas chos sprin char yang phab//ji ltar rgyal ba gdan la bzhugs   
65b8(1): snang ba|/de bzhin rdul sbubs ma lus kun na'ang ste//'on kyang bde gshegs (gshegs)dang 'pho ba med//zhing rnams kun tu yul yang rab tu ston//sa'i tshul la 'jug pa tshad 
66a1(1): ka med de//spyod pa thub pas byang chub sems dpa' bstan//bsam gyis mi khyab tshul ni kun gsungs pas//rgyal sras rnams ni chos kyi dbyings la 'jug/chos
66a2(1): dbyings 'jug tshul rnam pa rgya che ste//mtha' yas phyogs su mtha' dang dbus med sgo//rdul 'phran sbubs su rgyal ba 'phrul ba spros//'gro ba'i bsam pa kun kyang rab tu
66a3(1): mkhyen//phyogs rnams thams cad zhing rnams kun tu yang //'jig rten gtso mtshan 'gro dang mnyam bar byung //thams cad tshul skor sangs rgyas don yod de//'dul bas
66a4(1): sems can thams cad rnam par spyod//rdul sbubs kun tu mtha' dang dbus med pa'i//rnam 'phrul yangs pa rang byung shin tu ston//sngan chad thub pa'i byang chub spyod pa rnams//
66a5(1): ji bzhin kun kyang snying po byang chub brjod//yangs dus bskal pa ji snyed yod pa rnams//skad cig skad cig sangs rgyas ma lus ston//der ni sems can byung zhing 'jig

91b2(1): rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad yongs su dag par bya ba dang /smon lam rgya mtsho thams cad rab tu bsgrags pa'i sgo dag nas yang dgapar skul ba byed par gyur to/ /sems 
91b3(1): kyi skad cig re re la 'jigarten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyis 'gro ba thams cad kyi nges pa'i tshig dang /sangs rgyas kyi gsung gi yan lag rnam par bstan cing brjod pa'i sgo

91b8(1): kyi skad cig rere la 'jigarten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi 'khor kyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho thams cad na/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po rab tu gnas

93a8(1): pa'i snying po zhes bya ba rab tu bkye'o//'od zer rin po che thams cad kyi kha dog sgron ma'i sprin du bdal pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du rab tu snang ba de byung nas/tshul thams cad

94a1(1): @#//rgyan spos rin po che thams cad las byas pa/snying po 'dzam bu'i chu klung gi gser gyi tshon gyis rab tu mdzes par bris pa/'od kyi dkyil 'khor rnam par dag cing 'od

112a7(1): rgya mtsho la rab tu gnas so //'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho la la ni/'jig rten gyi dbang po thams cad kyi lus kyi gzugs brnyan rgya mtsho la rab tu gnasaso//'jig rten gyi khams rgyamatsho 
112a8(1): la la ni࿒ byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi lus rgya mtsho la rab tu gnas so/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho ma la ni/byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i smon lam kyis byung ba'i rgyan

112b2(1): rten rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos/yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho rab tu gnas pa/shin tu bstan pa'i don 'di 
112b3(1): nyid yongasu bstan pa'i phyir sangs rgyas kyi mthus/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//kun kyang zhing rnams rgya mtsho 'di dag rin chen rang bzhin dri med pa//rnam dag sna tshogs 
112b4(1): nor bu kun 'bar 'od zer rgya mtsho'i 'od ldan ltos//'di dag rgyal ba'i byin brlabs rgyu yis dbyibs legs 'khor bar snang/de dag gis ni phyogs bcu'i nam mkha'i dbyings rnams 
112b5(1): gzhi kun rab tu khyab//zhing rnams rgya mtsho la la dri med 'od can nam mkha'i dbyings la gnas//snang ba'i snying po'i 'od can nor bu'i rgya mtsho dag la legs par gnas//'gro ba de dbus 
112b6(1): rgyal ba 'khor la chos kyi 'khor lo bzang po skor//'di ni sangs rgyas yul yin sems can rgya chen shin tu mgu byed pa'o//la la me tog sgron ma kun rgyas pa'i mu tig nor bus brgyan//
112b7(1): 'od zer phra ba'i kun nas sprin 'bar spos 'phro sprin gyi 'od can te/zhing mang mtha' dang dbus med rgya chen pad ma'i rgya mtsho dag la gnas//sangs rgyas rnams kyis rang bzhin zhing rnam(sa) 
112b8(1): 'byam klas kun kyang rab tu mdzes//zhing rnams rgya mtsho dkyil 'khor dbyings rnams la la sangs rgyas byin la gnas//yangs shing rin po chen mang ldan rgya chen de dag byang chub   
113a1(1): @#/ O /sems dpas gang//lag na rdo rje rgya mtsho mang tshogs lha dbang lus la de bzhin gnas//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang ba mdzad kyi 'khor lo rtag 
113a2(1): tu 'khor ࿒࿒nor bu'i shing dang spos kyi 'od 'phro'i sprin rnams mnyam pa'i dbyings la gnas//chu'i phung po dag dang rdo rje rgya mtsho brtan la'ang de bzhin gnas//la la gos dang phrag dang rgyal mtshan 
113a3(1): me tog rgya mtsho dag la gnas//kun na'ang rnam 'phrul 'di dag rgya mchen rnam par snang mdzad ston pa mdzad//zhing rnams ring dang thung dang klong gi gzugs dang rnam par sna tshogs te//sna tshogs

113b1(1): kun la kun tu bzang po rtag par rab tu sgrub byed cing //de na bskal pa rgya mtshor 'gro ba smin par bya phyir spyad pa spyod//chos dbyings rgyas par bkang ste de dag kun tu g-i rnam 'phrul rgya che/
